(**
 *  Convert a string to Shift JIS character encoding.
 *  @param stri String to be converted.
 *  @return A Shift JIS encoded string.
 *  @exception RANGE_ERROR A character of ''stri'' cannot be converted.
 *)
const func string: toShiftJIS (in string: stri) is func
  result
    var string: shiftJIS is "";
  local
    var char: ch is ' ';
  begin
    block
      for ch range stri do
        shiftJIS &:= toShiftJIS[ch];
      end for;
    exception
      catch INDEX_ERROR:
        raise RANGE_ERROR;
    end block
  end func;


const type: fromShiftJisTable is hash[integer] char;

    [16#ea9f : '\22575;'], [16#eaa0 : '\27079;'], [16#eaa1 : '\36953;'], [16#eaa2 : '\29796;'],
    [16#eaa3 : '\20956;'], [16#eaa4 : '\29081;']);


(**
 *  Convert Shift JIS encoded string to a Unicode string.
 *  @param shiftJIS String with Shift JIS encoding.
 *  @return A Unicode string which corresponds to ''shiftJIS''.
 *  @exception RANGE_ERROR If ''shiftJIS'' is not a Shift JIS encoded.
 *)
const func string: fromShiftJIS (in string: shiftJIS) is func
  result
    var string: stri is "";
  local
    var integer: index is 1;
    var char: ch1 is ' ';
    var integer: charNumber is 0;
  begin
    block
      while index <= length(shiftJIS) do
        ch1 := shiftJIS[index];
        if ch1 >= '\16#81;' and ch1 <= '\16#9f;' or
            ch1 >= '\16#e0;' and ch1 <= '\16#ef;' then
          if index < length(shiftJIS) then
            charNumber := ord(ch1) * 256 + ord(shiftJIS[succ(index)]);
            index +:= 2;
          else
            raise RANGE_ERROR;
          end if;
        else
          charNumber := ord(ch1);
          incr(index);
        end if;
        stri &:= fromShiftJIS[charNumber];
      end while;
    exception
      catch INDEX_ERROR:
        raise RANGE_ERROR;
    end block
  end func;
